# Daily delivery-step audit: 2026-11-04 UTC

Window: **2026-11-04T00:00:00.000Z ≤ event time < 2026-11-05T00:00:00.000Z**.
Evidence: consistent SQLite backup completed at 2026-11-05T00:24:12.782179Z through a read-only
connection to the controller database; maximum flow sequence 168041, event time 2026-11-05T00:22:01.633Z.
Reader source: `cb7a30ffe4e58b2f23d11eacddf2301ddd595eee`. No clone code was installed, built or run.

Recommend **one transactional landing state/telemetry writer**. All 623 successful dev landings lose their
measured landing interval to premature closure; each of 908 landing requests has two enqueue records.

## Daily measurements and limits

The appendix preserves the complete JSON from this telemetry extraction against the private backup,
with `GOAL_MAIN_CHECKOUT` set to this worktree and `GOAL_LOCAL_CONFIG` / `GOAL_STATE_DIRECTORY`
pointing into its `.temp/daily-step-audit/`. It was not the deferred acceptance run.

```sh
bun tools/goal/src/cli.ts report --flow --since 2026-11-04T00:00:00.000Z --json
```

This command has no end bound: the raw export includes November 5 events through the backup and current task
states. Its lifecycle `asOf` is 2026-11-05T00:25:23.612Z; its trailing hour starts at November 4 23:25:23.612Z.
The guard ledger uses its own seven-day window and registered guard identities, independently of `--since`;
its zero firings do not establish zero daily refusals. The daily table instead filters `readFlow` at both bounds,
passes `readFlowTasks` without capture-time `terminalState` to `flowReport`, and uses midnight as `now`.

| Recorded interval | Completed pairs | Sum | p90 |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Dispatch → start request | 0 | unknown | unknown |
| Start request → running receipt | 1,000 | 148.758 s | 187 ms |
| Worker started → finished | 978 | 700.007 h | 98.547 min |
| Landing enqueued → finished | 279 | 7.957 h | 200.872 s |
| Check / dev-update / verification / integration / promotion | 0 each | unknown | unknown |

These overlapping elapsed/residency intervals are neither CPU time nor billable time. Missing, closed and
cross-midnight pairs are excluded. Dispatch uses attempt zero while start uses worker attempts. The landing
sample contains 253 refusals and 26 successful non-dev outcomes, excluding all 623 dev successes.
All five bounded event queues read zero, but historical queue membership is unknown; missing producers and
closures preclude treating these as proof of empty queues. The reader's interval ratio is 0.8287089564317679
and launches/integrated ratio is 1.4600638977635783; incomplete waits and resumes prevent calling these
productive efficiency or distinct attempts per delivered task.

## Steps, catches, cost and disposition

**Allowance cost is unknown for every row.** Pooled before/after allowance observations and attempt token totals
do not attribute consumption to pipeline substeps. Unique prevented-defect counts are also unknown: the
recorded refusal/failure observations below can include infrastructure failures and repeated defects.
`hostMs` is a terminal landing envelope, including earlier work; substep time is unknown unless stated.
Envelopes and flow intervals overlap and must not be added together.

| Step | Failure it prevents | Recorded catches in the day | Recorded time; decision |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Dispatch: contract, owner, dependency and risk admission | Invalid or premature work | Refusals unknown; 661 dispatch admissions | unknown; **keep** |
| Allowance/capacity admission, dependency setup and launch | Exhaustion, oversubscription or invalid source/install | Preventive catches unknown; 1,014 start requests and 13 launch-failed events | Only running-receipt interval above; full setup unknown; **keep** |
| Worker implementation, local typecheck and focused tests | Incomplete behavior and regressions | Local catches unknown; 87 exit classifications: 76 environment, 10 pool-exhausted, 1 spec-conflict | 700.007 residency h; work/check/wait split unknown; **keep** |
| Shared-check slot request/acquisition/release | Overloaded checks or leaked custody | unknown; no check-stage flow events | unknown; **keep** |
| Result schema, source/attempt fence and landing enqueue | Stale, dirty or uncommitted source | Schema catches unknown; 6 dirty-source and 1 no-commit refusal recorded later under rebase | Substep unknown; **keep guards, merge transition writers** |
| Train claim, candidate preparation and formatting | Invalid candidate or unparseable source | unknown; no setup-stage terminal refusals recorded | unknown; **keep** |
| Git reconciliation and derived-file regeneration | Unresolved or stale generated source | 186 further rebase refusals: 181 Git reconciliation, 1 JSON conflict, 3 semantic retention, 1 regeneration | All 193 rebase-terminal envelopes: 1,248.372 s; split unknown; **keep** |
| Scope/risk and source/contract proof binding | Unauthorized edits or proof for another source | 3 undeclared-risk refusals; separate binding catches unknown | Scope-terminal envelopes: 9.914 s; **keep** |
| Conditional prelanding compiler/boot and main/control-plane gates | Broken compiler, behavior or control plane | 9 gate refusals: 8 compiler failures and 1 other gate failure; unique product defects unknown | Gate-terminal envelopes: 533.631 s; per-command split unknown; **keep existing placement** |
| Merge freshness, semantic retention and dev ref update | Lost edits, no-effect changes or stale publication | 48 merge refusals: 45 semantic conflicts, 3 no-effect results; 623 dev updates | Refusal envelopes: 200.034 s; all 649 successes: 4,765.184 s; **keep checks, merge completion writes** |
| Independent post-dev verification | Undetected integrated regressions | 947 stored command results: 570 pass, 377 fail; 93 finding rows created: 13 new/fail, 80 unknown/infrastructure | Command durations unknown; **keep** |
| Integration settlement and Goal completion | Declaring undelivered or unproved work complete | Separate catches unknown; 649 integration events are settlement receipts, not proof of verification | unknown; **keep** |
| Promotion admission, full gates and main compare-and-swap | Publishing unqualified source or overwriting main | unknown; no promotion-stage flow events | unknown; **keep**; capture-time `stabilize-blocked` is not a daily catch count |

There are 902 `gate-outcome` rows for 902 distinct task/attempt pairs: 649 pass and 253 fail
(193 rebase + 3 scope + 9 gate + 48 merge). These are attempt outcomes, not unique tasks or defects.
Verifier results are separate durable evidence despite absent verification flow events; their failures
are not all new regressions and cannot be summed with landing refusals. Boot/typecheck placement is conditional,
as documented in [Goal integration](../README.md#integration-promotion-culprit-isolation-and-attempt-fencing);
the daily events do not identify which optional checks ran.

## One structural change and the measure

Consolidate queue insertion, landing state settlement and terminal flow emission behind one idempotent SQLite
transition keyed by a stable landing execution identity. Emit enqueue on actual insertion and successful completion
before generic terminal cleanup, with replay returning the existing result. Preserve source fencing, cancellation
and all gate boundaries. This is a design recommendation; adoption belongs in the owning Goal document.

Current split: [store enqueue](../src/store.ts) and [queue enqueue/finish](../src/land.ts) both emit flow;
`WorkerStore.saveTask` closes pending flow before `SqliteLandingQueue.finish` records completion.
[The flow reader](../src/flow.ts) correctly discards closed intervals. All 623 dev-success pairs have a closure
before finish. Every one of the 908 in-day enqueue identities has exactly two events, totaling 1,816.

Measure **successful dev landing executions with exactly one matched interval / successful dev landing executions**:
baseline **0/623**. Target **100% for two full UTC days**, resolving cross-midnight starts from prior history,
including replay, and never converting cancellation to success. Require **one enqueue per execution**
(baseline **1,816/908 = 2**), with unchanged gate outcomes on replay. Measure transition latency and per-execution
allowance attribution before claiming throughput or allowance savings; those marginal costs are currently unknown.
The previous audit recommended the same consolidation; today's durable evidence shows the measurement defect persists.

Primary-source patterns, inspected from existing sparse clones with `git show`, support the proposed design:

- **Temporal Server**, `.reference/delivery-tooling/temporal/`, commit `3eb5fb80523443d8583bcec463843275908f1bbc`,
  **MIT**, `LICENSE`: [`service/history/workflow/mutable_state_impl.go:7756–7800`](https://github.com/temporalio/temporal/blob/3eb5fb80523443d8583bcec463843275908f1bbc/service/history/workflow/mutable_state_impl.go#L7756)
  returns a conditional state mutation together with workflow events. Apply transaction-bound history to Bun/SQLite;
  this is not a recommendation to adopt Temporal or copy its implementation.
- **OpenTelemetry semantic conventions**, `.reference/delivery-tooling/semantic-conventions/`, commit
  `7b31585fe9661d855b7f1745722aa20979eaf847`, **Apache-2.0**, `LICENSE`:
  [`docs/cicd/cicd-spans.md:115–150`](https://github.com/open-telemetry/semantic-conventions/blob/7b31585fe9661d855b7f1745722aa20979eaf847/docs/cicd/cicd-spans.md#L115)
  specifies task-run identity, result and error type. Use those concepts in durable flow records; an exporter is optional.

Clone paths above are relative to `/home/edge/projects/rezics/rezics-next/`; its `.reference/INDEX.md` reuse dates
were updated. Neither upstream source supplies REZICS catch counts or proves any savings.

Daily extraction uses `flow_events.at` and `worker_events.at` with both bounds. Outcome fields are
`gate-outcome.body.{stage,passed,hostMs,firstFailure.assertion}`; exit classes use
`attempt-exit-classified.body.kind`. Verifier rows use `created_at` in the same window:
`dev_verifier_runs.body.evidence.{passed,result}` and `dev_verifier_findings.{status,body.result}`.
These are durable rows, not estimates from transient logs.

Decision: use the contract-named report path and UTC-bounded snapshot totals alongside the complete open-ended export.
Acceptance, native/browser, root, worker-check and remote-check runs are deferred to integration. No tests changed.
